Injected faults on device results, and correctness before performance (GV-003 "Match reference decisions and verifier outcomes", scenario "Stable malformed solver output"; GV-006 "Correctness precedes performance acceptance"; GV-001 for the launch attribution; Task 5)
Bend version: bend 2.0.39 (driver sha256 2ec160fa4486d33aff51e9eedcc294d6b531dc10c279c00b0c00685dae2045cd)
Compiler: clang version 23.1.1 at /usr/bin/clang (GPU build: emitted C with -DBEND_CUDA=1 at -O3, per verification/gpu-toolchain.txt); NVRTC 13.4.92 compiles each device program; compute-sanitizer 2026.3.0.0 is the launch observer
Host: Linux 7.2.9-1-MANJARO x86_64, 32 online CPUs; 1-minute load average 23.48 at build time and 19.50 to 32.63 in the runs' load_avg lines; the display and other graphics and compute clients share the GPU; every wall time is indicative only and is not a performance measurement
GPU: NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4090, GPU-0188dad3-4d11-4254-3a0d-50e3808bf464, bus 00000000:0C:00.0, compute capability 8.9 (cubins sm=89), driver 615.71.09, CUDA UMD 13.4
Date: 2026-11-05 04:40 to 04:45 UTC (2026-11-04 CST)
Environment: export PATH="$HOME/.local/share/writ-toolchains/bend-2.0.39/bend/bin:/opt/cuda/bin:$PATH"; export BEND_NO_TELEMETRY=1; export CUDA_HOME=/opt/cuda. CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES unset and BENDRTS unset in every run (every record states it). Commands run from the worktree root. The probes were uncommitted at capture (each record's lane_commit is 843570f marked "uncommitted: M scripts/gpu-validation/..."); their sha256 in every record equals the committed files: Solver.bend 01972f7ec80cdb26f4b1758f965483989d1a95cdc0183a6d490ceaf920ab8669, Labels.bend 4c5c97b7fbeb6d506d11249422395604fb7b758740425dbd927b1790e3db08ce, Transfers.bend 6b45e9bfcef902eb577ba02367b35196edd114f1954e743104a1f00dda6ecbaa.
Execution: device runs, observed: the observed (compute-sanitizer) pass of every observe.sh run below with a nonzero bend_dev count, `+RTS --gpu on -RTS`. Device runs, not individually observed: the plain pass of those runs. CPU runs: every `--gpu off` comparison run; the CPU reference inside every run (Core.solve_cpu, Dlm.batch, Transfer.batch without `!`); every library check applied to an injected value (Core.verify, Core.authorize_sink, Dlm.verify_batch, Transfer.admit, DataOS.commit_from_capability), which are CPU code by design; the transfers digest controls; and the forged `labels bench-forged` run, which dispatches nothing to the device (0 launches).

Probes: scripts/gpu-validation/Solver.bend, Labels.bend and Transfers.bend gain mode `inject CASE [ROUNDS]` (CASE is `all` or one inject case name; ROUNDS repeats the device call on the same inputs, each repeat behind its own IO.now() step), and Labels.bend gains `bench-forged SIZE REPS`. Run and parity modes are unchanged. One GpuLibrary entry and one bang site per binary (BANGS 1 each, below). No library module changed: every fault is injected by the probe, after the device call returns and before the check. All inputs are SYNTHETIC and are marked so in the output.

Per inject case, each probe runs the device path ROUNDS times on the case's base inputs, then an IO step, then the CPU reference, and prints:
- the last device result, the CPU reference, and the last device result after injection, every field;
- `control CASE`: every unmutated device result equals the CPU reference and the independent check accepts it (so the refusal that follows is caused by the injection, and the check is not an always-refuse);
- the library function's return value on the injected value, quoted (`verifier`, `replay` lines), and the first field where the injected value and the independent replay differ (`replay difference`); for the solver also a `clauses` line evaluating each original-graph check separately;
- `inject CASE`: PASS only if the library path refuses every injected result (k of n rounds);
- the effect check: solver `effect CASE` (Core.authorize_sink with a SYNTHETIC sink refuses with the same reason, so no sink is authorized); transfers forged-commit `commit`/`effect` lines (DataOS.commit_from_capability returns Rejected with the exact Running session and held ledger, no WriteIntent; its twin with the read grant live commits). Dlm.verify_batch and Transfer.admit return a Bool and a Decision; no library entry point takes an answer tree or a Decision as authority, so those cases have no effect to emit.
The injections, exactly:
- solver (base: parity graph wide-dimensions, node 0 with bits 31, 32, 63 in secrecy, untrusted and effects, flow 0->1, calls 0->2, 2 sweeps):
  - drop-node: node 2 (the last) removed from the candidate's node list, 3 nodes -> 2;
  - erase-source: node 0's secrecy 2147483648/2147483649 -> 0/0 (the source fact the seed carries);
  - clear-bit63: node 2's effects hi word 2147483649 -> 1 (bit 63 cleared; node 2 inherits it along calls 0->2);
  - stale-stamp: the candidate stamp's authority epoch 3 -> 2.
- labels (base: parity queries allow-deny, answers T F T T):
  - flip-denial: leaf root.left.right (internal->public, denied) False -> True;
  - truncate: the tree replaced by its left half (4 leaves -> 2);
  - swap-shape: ((a b) (c d)) -> (a (b (c d))), leaf order unchanged, so a leaf-only comparison would accept it.
- transfers:
  - fabricate-admitted (base: parity missing-read, 2011 admitted, 2012 without its read grant): root.right Denied Read -> a fabricated Admitted ticket for plan 2012 with the Scenario output policy;
  - wrong-plan (base: paired-admitted 2001, 2002): root.left's ticket plan replaced by plan 2002, invocation 2001 -> 2002, output policy unchanged;
  - stale-context (base: 2041, 2042 admitted at live stamp 1.2.3): the device tree is presented unchanged against the authoritative Context for 2041 whose live stamp has advanced to 1.2.4 (authority epoch 3 -> 4);
  - forged-commit (base: the Scenario plan 99 with Scenario.transfer() and with Scenario.without_read()): root.right Denied Read -> a fabricated Admitted ticket for plan 99, then the commit path below.
- Commit path: DataOS.commit_from_capability has no Decision parameter; it re-resolves the capability (Frontend.checked_plan re-runs Transfer.admit) and Backplane.commit re-admits against the live transfer Context. So the forged decision itself cannot be handed to it. The probe routes the forged request's exact plan (99) and the authoritative Context that leaf was evaluated against (the Scenario negotiation with the read grant withdrawn) through it, from the SYNTHETIC Running session and held ledger (replicated from examples/Commit.bend). Transfer.admit on that Context returns Denied Read, and the commit returns Rejected.
- Digest sensitivity controls (every transfers inject run): Transfers.hash of admitted ticket 2001 must change when any one of 20 fields changes (every Plan field, every OutputPolicy field, the origin count, and each origin field including approved codes and payment), and the ticket rebuilt field by field must hash equal. A `transfers run 16384` run executes the all-field digest on device output in run mode.
- Wrong-but-faster control: `labels bench-forged SIZE REPS` builds run mode's inputs and runs run mode's checks (Labels.check), but replaces the device call with a constant allow-all tree of the same shape. No bang runs. It is compared with two honest `labels run 16384 200` runs (before and after).

Harness: scripts/gpu-validation/observe.sh and check-record.sh, unchanged since Task 2 (sha256 efd08afb...a8ac and 7d4df17b...88ab in every record). The sanitizer form is the controller's ruling: `compute-sanitizer --dump-kernel-launches --print-limit 0 --kernel-name-exclude regex=.* <binary> +RTS --gpu on -RTS <args>`, plus observe.sh's `--print-session-details --log-file`. Each observe.sh run writes a plain/observed record pair inline below. The plain record's `exit_status` is the probe's, the observed record's is compute-sanitizer's, and the `[exit status]` after observe.sh is check-record.sh's gate verdict (0 PASS, 1 FAIL). Task 7's `bench` measurement does not exist yet; the forged run is measured by the same harness Task 7 will use for its cold runs (observe.sh plain-pass wall_ms and the gate).

Unrecorded calibration runs (activity.log, 2026-11-05T04:41:26Z): before the recorded runs, eight plain `--gpu on` runs of the task5 binaries, outside observe.sh, chose ROUNDS. Their stdout was discarded, so only the exit status and wall time were seen. Exit status alone tells whether a probe printed a FAIL line, because a probe exits 1 on any FAIL. The runs:
- `solver inject drop-node 100`: exit 0, 604 ms;
- `labels inject flip-denial 200`: exit 0, 494 ms;
- `transfers inject wrong-plan 200`: exit 0, 427 ms;
- `labels run 16384 200`: exit 0, 2414 ms;
- `labels bench-forged 16384 200`: exit 1, 998 ms (the forged control, expected to FAIL);
- `solver inject drop-node 1000`: exit 0, 4437 ms;
- `labels inject flip-denial 2000`: exit 0, 4334 ms;
- `transfers inject forged-commit 2000`: exit 0, 3888 ms.
During development, the probes were also run on the Bend interpreter (`bend scripts/gpu-validation/X.bend inject ...` and `bench-forged 16 2`). Every inject case printed only PASS lines there, and the forged run printed its FAIL lines. None of these runs is evidence. Their outputs were not kept, and no recorded run below was repeated to change an outcome.

## Build
Each probe is built twice from the worktree root to ../writ-build/physical-gpu/task5/ (a new directory, so the Task 3 and Task 4 binaries and their recorded hashes are untouched): first to C (for the BANGS line and emitted_c_sha256), then to the binary, whose build runs the GPU compile and writes NAME.gpu beside it. Transfers.bend now also imports Dlm, Negotiation, Ledger, Frontend, Backplane and DataOS (for the commit path); its emitted C still has BANGS 1.

## Run

Run directories: ../writ-build/physical-gpu/runs/task5/<run>/. Order: solver inject cases (ROUNDS 500), labels inject cases (ROUNDS 1000), transfers inject cases (ROUNDS 1000), transfers run 16384 (digest), then labels honest, forged, honest (the wrong-but-faster control, no --gpu off twin). Each inject section has the observe.sh record pair, the plain stdout, the stdout and launch-count checks, and its `--gpu off` comparison run with two diffs.

  leaf 1: allowed False
  leaf 2: allowed True
  leaf 3: allowed True

## Result
On this host and configuration only (device GPU-0188dad3-4d11-4254-3a0d-50e3808bf464, driver 615.71.09, bend 2.0.39, NVRTC/CUDA 13.4.92; solver.gpu d9fe90a9aab566ad8e13b62baa34dc8b5d3c1bb20912b5736d17b0c26c3a8928, labels.gpu b489ef9f527e76b89fcbd878210469cb2c717426261e85a399f7710ee850cecb, transfers.gpu b1a4d2a7662e05ff31293c6f1699f2cb10393d959f9258c52b925afabc8ffa37).

Cases injected: 11. Cases denied: 11 (every round of each). Not denied: none. Each run's unmutated control passed, its `--gpu off` stdout is byte-identical to the `--gpu on` plain-pass and observed-pass stdout, and check-record.sh returns 0 on its record.

Digest sensitivity (Task 4's limit): in all four transfers inject runs, all 20 `digest control` lines PASS (each one-field change moves Transfers.hash from 2938088844 to a different value) and the field-by-field rebuild hashes equal. `transfers run 16384` (5 launches, gate PASS) executes the all-field digest on device output: device and CPU both print hash 1820298339 (admitted 8192, denied Read 4096, denied Write 4096), and `--gpu off` stdout is identical. This closes "the revised digest is never executed" for this binary. It does not make the digest equality evidence (finding below).

This task provides the label and transfer verifier-denial controls that gpu-paths.txt and gpu-parity.txt lacked (Task 3 review finding 2; the Task 4 limit). Dlm.verify_batch refuses 1000 of 1000 rounds in each of three cases, and the Transfer.admit replay refuses 1000 of 1000 rounds in each of four. Each of these cases also has an unmutated control that the same check accepts.

Wrong-but-faster control (GV-006):

| Run | Args | Launches | Plain wall_ms | correctness | Gate | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| labels-honest-1 | run 16384 200 | 1000 | 3208 | PASS 6, FAIL 0 | PASS | accepted (correct) |
| labels-forged | bench-forged 16384 200 | 0 | 1164 | PASS 3, FAIL 3 | FAIL (exit_status 1 in both passes; correctness not all PASS in both; 0 bend_dev launches) | FAILED, wall time not accepted |
| labels-honest-2 | run 16384 200 | 1000 | 3211 | PASS 6, FAIL 0 | PASS | accepted (correct) |

The forged run is faster (1164 ms against 3208 and 3211 ms, loaded host) and wrong: "device answer tree equals the CPU reference" 0 of 200, "match the expected allow/deny pattern" 0 of 200, and "pass Dlm.verify_batch" 0 of 200 all FAIL. The probe exits 1. check-record.sh returns 1 and the record says gate FAIL. Its 1164 ms is recorded here only as the wall time of a failed run. It is not a performance number and must appear in Task 7 as rejected.

Finding (a digest collision, recorded, not explained away): in the forged run the line "device answer digest equals the CPU reference digest" PASSED (200 of 200). The constant allow-all tree of 16384 leaves and the correct tree (8192 allowed, 8192 denied) have the same Labels.hash, 2954464355. Labels.hash is a mod-2^32 polynomial with multiplier 31 (word 2 allowed, 1 denied). The leaves that differ are exactly the odd-numbered ones. The collision is structural, not tied to one size: the Task 5 reviewer rebuilt Labels.hash offline and found that the correct alternating tree and the allow-all tree hash equal at every power-of-two size from 256 leaves up to 1048576. They differ only from 2 to 128 leaves. At 16384 both give 2954464355, which reproduces the recorded value. Consequence: no 32-bit digest in these probes is equality evidence. The comparison of record is the typed in-process comparison, Solver.result_equal, Dlm.answers_equal and Transfers.Decisions.equal, together with the per-leaf expected outcomes. The Task 3 cross-process label comparison at 16384 still differed in the printed counts ("allowed 16384, denied 0" against "allowed 8192, denied 8192"), so a stdout diff would have caught this case, but the hash alone would not. The in-process typed comparison (Dlm.answers_equal), the expected-pattern check and Dlm.verify_batch all refused the forged tree. Solver.hash and Transfers.hash use the same multiplier-31 construction and are not shown collision-resistant either. The 20 transfers digest controls show single-field sensitivity on one ticket only.

No hardware coverage (cases whose launch count was 0):
- labels-forged (`bench-forged 16384 200`): 0 launches, by construction. It is the forged control, not a device case.
Every injected case had 5000 attributed launches. The library checks applied to the injected values, the digest controls and the commit path are CPU code in every run; the device produced only the base results that the probe then corrupted.

## Limits
- One host, one GPU, one driver, one Bend and CUDA version. The display and other compute clients shared the GPU. Load average was 19.5 to 32.6 during the runs. Wall times, including the wrong-but-faster comparison, are indicative only on a loaded host.
- The injections are the 11 listed, each on a small synthetic base (2 to 4 nodes or leaves). They show that each named library path refuses these specific corruptions. They are not a search over corruptions. No fault is injected inside the device, the runtime or managed memory, and no bit flip in transit is simulated.
- Each injection is a probe-side change of a value in the same process. The checks are logical, in the same address space. They are not a protection boundary.
- Dlm.verify_batch and the transfer replay are the same evaluators as the CPU reference (Dlm.batch plus answers_equal; Transfer.admit). Their refusals here show they are not always-accept, and the controls show they are not always-refuse; they do not show an algorithm independent of the producer. Core.verify is a separate algorithm (an original-graph closure check), and the clauses line shows which of its checks refused.
- Core.sink_gate (an exported helper, not an entry point) is called only to print, as a diagnostic, that the erase-source and clear-bit63 corruptions would have widened authority if verification were skipped. Its value is never used.
- The commit path cannot take a forged Decision: it re-derives admission from the authoritative Context. The forged-commit case therefore shows that the commit refuses the forged request's plan against the Context the forged leaf was evaluated against. It does not show a decision value being rejected at commit, because no such input exists. The commit runs once per process, not once per device round, on the SYNTHETIC Scenario session, ledger and registry. It runs on the CPU.
- Labels has no effect path to check. Dlm.verify_batch returns a Bool, and the commit path evaluates Dlm.flows itself.
- bench-forged exists in Labels only. Task 7's `bench` measurement does not exist yet, so the forged run is measured by observe.sh's plain-pass wall_ms, not by per-component IO.now timing. Task 7 must list it as rejected.
- No 32-bit digest in these probes is equality evidence (the structural collision above). The comparison of record is the typed in-process comparison (Solver.result_equal, Dlm.answers_equal, Transfers.Decisions.equal) plus the per-leaf expected outcomes.
- Consequently, the Task 3 cross-process `--gpu off` comparisons at SIZE 16384 and in the long runs (verification/gpu-paths.txt, the Limits bullet near line 1571: "counts and a 32-bit digest") show equal counts, not equal results, for all three probes. A difference that keeps the counts equal, such as two leaves swapped, would rest on the digest alone. gpu-paths.txt is not edited here; Task 9 reconciles the records. The in-process typed comparisons in those runs are unaffected.
- The calibration runs listed in the Harness section were not recorded and are not evidence.
- Hidden-device runs, default mode and `--gpu <size>` were not run for these modes. Device memory checking was not run (every kernel is excluded by design), and Nsight is absent.
- Pre-commit capture: every record's lane_commit is 843570f marked uncommitted for the three probes. Their source_sha256 values bind the records to the committed files listed in the header.
- Not run: `bend Tests.bend` and `bend PROOF.bend`. No library, law or proof source changed; only scripts/gpu-validation/*.bend.
